@intelliflow/project-tracker:build: This can slow down deployments or lead to failures when size limits are exceeded.
@intelliflow/project-tracker:build: To resolve this, you can
@intelliflow/project-tracker:build: - make sure the path is statically scoped to some subfolder, for example path.join(process.cwd(), 'data', bar), or
@intelliflow/project-tracker:build: - only use them in development, or
@intelliflow/project-tracker:build: - opt out by adding an ignore comment to the highlighted call: fs.existsSync(/*turbopackIgnore: true*/ ...), or
@intelliflow/project-tracker:build: - remove them.
